./validation/fullrun-lab/v3-poll-r2/apriori/review/quick-poll-req-review-v1-raw.jsonl:12:{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_3","type":"command_execution","command":"/usr/bin/zsh -lc \"sed -n '1,240p' requirement/req-v1.md\"","aggregated_output":"# 需求：快速投票小工具（quick-poll）\n\n> 目标线（lineage）：master（主线，空仓起步，无 merge 禁忌）\n> 来源：P13 头脑风暴共识（2026-07-08）。本文档是 STEP0 的 req-v1 起点，将经异构评审迭代收敛。\n\n## 1. 目标（Target State B）\n\n一个零门槛的网页投票小工具。发起人填「标题 + 若干选项」建出一个投票，拿到两条链接：**投票链接**（分享给大家）和**管理链接**（只有发起人持有，用于关闭/管理）。拿到投票链接的人**无需登录、匿名**、一次点击即可投票；投完立即看到实时结果。适用于开会、群里几十人快速征集意见。\n\n## 2. 目标用户与规模\n\n- **发起人**：想快速征集意见的人（会议主持、群主、组织者）。\n- **投票人**：拿到链接的同事/朋友，**匿名、不登录**，单次投票量级约几十人（数量级 10–100，非高并发）。\n\n## 3. 核心场景（每个用户可见输出对应一个带稳定 ID 的验收项）\n\n### 建投票\n- **PC-01 创建投票**：发起人提交「标题 + ≥2 个选项 + 投票模式（单选/多选）+ 可选截止时间」，系统创建投票并返回两条链接：投票链接、管理链接（后者含只有发起人拥有的密钥）。\n- **PC-02 输入校验**：标题为空、或有效选项少于 2 个 → 拒绝创建并给出可读错误，不生成投票。\n\n### 投票\n- **PC-03 投票（单选）**：单选投票下，投票人选中恰好一个选项并提交 → 该选项票数 +1，随后进入结果视图。\n- **PC-04 投票（多选）**：多选投票下，投票人选中一个或多个选项并提交 → 所选各选项票数各 +1。多选下\"未选任何项\"的提交被拒绝并提示。\n- **PC-05 浏览器软限重复投票**：同一浏览器对同一投票默认只记一次；已投后再次打开投票链接 → 直接进入结果视图而非投票视图（标记存于该浏览器本地，换设备/清缓存/无痕不受本工具阻止——这是刻意的零门槛取舍）。\n\n### 看结果\n- **PC-06 投完即看实时结果**：投票人提交后进入结果视图，展示各选项票数与总票数；结果视图每隔数秒自动刷新以反映他人新投的票（轮询，非推送）。\n\n### 关闭与生命周期\n- **PC-07 到点自动关闭**：设置了截止时间的投票，到达截止时间后进入「已关闭」状态；此后任何投票提交被拒绝并提示已结束，仍可查看结果。\n- **PC-08 发起人手动关闭**：持管理链接者可提前将投票置为「已关闭」；此后投票提交被拒绝，仍可查看结果。无有效管理密钥者无法关闭。\n- **PC-09 已关闭态可见**：投票与结果视图明确显示投票是否已关闭（以及关闭原因/截止信息），已关闭时不再展示投票操作。\n\n### 存储与并发（外部共享状态——三个时刻见 §6）\n- **PC-10 并发投票不丢票**：多名投票人几乎同时对同一投票提交时，所有合法票都被计入，不因读改写交叠而互相覆盖（文件存储下的并发安全是显式保证，见 §6 与验收）。\n- **PC-11 崩溃不产生半写文件**：一次写入过程中进程中断，投票数据文件不会被写坏（采用「写临时文件 + 原子 rename」；成功返回即已落盘）。\n\n## 4. 验收标准（可测，\"若…则…\"）\n\n- PC-01：若提交合法的标题+≥2 选项+模式，则返回含两条链接的响应，且投票链接与管理链接不同、管理链接含密钥。\n- PC-02：若标题为空或有效选项<2，则返回错误、HTTP 非 2xx，且未创建任何投票记录。\n- PC-03：若单选投票下提交恰好一个选项，则该选项计数 +1，响应导向结果视图。\n- PC-03b：若单选投票下提交了多于一个选项，则拒绝并提示。\n- PC-04：若多选投票下提交 k(k≥1) 个选项，则这 k 个选项各 +1；k=0 时拒绝并提示。\n- PC-05：若同一浏览器已对某投票投过票，则再次访问投票链接直接呈现结果视图。\n- PC-06：若投票成功，则结果视图显示各选项票数与总数；页面每 N 秒（N 可配置，默认约 3s）自动拉取一次最新结果。\n- PC-07：若当前时间 ≥ 截止时间，则投票状态为已关闭，提交投票返回错误（非 2xx），结果仍可读。\n- PC-08：若持有效管理密钥请求关闭，则状态变为已关闭；若无有效密钥请求关闭，则被拒绝（非 2xx），状态不变。\n- PC-09：若投票已关闭，则投票/结果视图显式呈现「已关闭」，且不渲染投票提交控件。\n- PC-10：若对同一投票并发发起多次合法投票，则最终各选项计数等于各自合法提交次数之和（无丢失）。\n- PC-11：若在写入投票文件过程中模拟中断，则文件要么是旧完整内容、要么是新完整内容，绝不为半写损坏内容。\n\n## 5. 非目标（明确不做，附原因）\n\n- **账号 / 登录 / 注册**——要的就是零门槛匿名。\n- **IP 防刷票**——同一办公室/WiFi 出口 IP 相同会误伤同事，弊大于利。\n- **改票**（投后修改）——简单、够用；避免计数回滚复杂度。\n- **建成后修改标题/选项**——已投的票会与改后选项对不上。\n- **结果导出（CSV 等）**——v1 用不上。\n- **结果可见性开关（投票期间隐藏结果 / 关闭后揭晓）**——用得少，默认「投完即看」。\n- **实时推送（WebSocket）**——几十人量级轮询即可，避免额外复杂度。\n- **投票数据自动过期/垃圾回收**——v1 先不做生命周期清理。\n- **数据库（含 SQLite）**——发起人明确不希望为此装 DB；采用文件存储（并发安全由实现保证，见 §6）。\n\n## 6. 外部共享状态的三个时刻（文件存储）\n\n投票数据是跨多个投票人共享的外部状态，需描述：\n- **初始化**：创建投票时生成唯一投票 ID + 管理密钥，落一个投票数据文件（标题、选项、模式、截止时间、初始计数=0、状态=open）。\n- **运行时更新**：每次投票 / 关闭操作，在单一 Node 进程内用**串行化写队列（按投票 ID 加锁）**读改写，保证不交叠覆盖（PC-10）；写盘用**临时文件 + 原子 rename**（PC-11）。\n- **清理 / 失效**：v1 无自动清理（列为非目标）；「已关闭」为逻辑失效标志，数据仍保留可读。\n\n## 7. 技术方案（brainstorm 选定）\n\n单个 Node 服务；**文件存储（无数据库）**；服务端渲染页面 + 少量前端 JS；结果视图轮询刷新。并发安全按 §6 处理。\n\n## 8. 开放问题（交 STEP0 评审收敛）\n\n- 截止时间的时区/格式约定（服务器本地时间 vs UTC；输入格式）。\n- 管理密钥的强度与长度、是否可通过管理链接查看更详细统计。\n- 选项数量上限、标题/选项长度上限（防滥用/超长输入）。\n- 「已关闭」后结果页是否长期可访问（与\"无自动过期\"一致，默认是）。\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
  if (!opts.change) return err(res, 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]');
  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(opts.change)) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' — bare kebab-case only`);
  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
  res.stage = loc.stage;
  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
  let state;
  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || configTestCmd(cwd);
  if (!testCmd) return err(res, 'no test command: pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md');

  const b = checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, testCmd);
  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
  res.checks.push(b.check);
  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
  if (rdDefect) {
    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
  } else {
    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
  }

  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));

  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : 'PASS';
  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : 0;
  return res;
}

function toJson(res) {
  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
}

const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]';

function cli(argv) {
  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {

    const res = runGate(a);
    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
    if (res.code !== 2) console.log(res.code === 0 ? `\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}` : `\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
    return res.code;
  });
}

module.exports = { runGate, resolveChange, classifyStatus, cli };

# Changelog

All notable changes to `apriori-cli`. Versions follow semver; the stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format and the flow-state schema only break in a major.

## 4.0.0 — 2026-07-12 · the change bundle

Everything a change produces lives in ONE directory: `apriori/changes/<name>/` — `flow-state.md`, the `requirement/` history (plain names: `req-v{N}.md`, `req-final.md`, `intent-card.md`), `gap-report.md`, `proposal.md`, `design.md`, `tasks.md`, the `specs/` deltas, the `review/` evidence (ledger `issues.md`, review docs, raws), and `spike/` on the explore track. The five scattered per-change roots (`requirement/`, `spike/`, `apriori/review/`, `apriori/design/`, `apriori/explore/`) cease to exist.

- **The archive move carries the whole bundle** — `apriori archive` moves the change dir in one atomic rename with everything already inside; the 3.4.x staging phase is deleted. Explore-track `spike/` is deleted or quarantined by the executor *before* the archive action (the command never touches it).
- **Gates read the bundle** — C4's ledger lives at `<dir>/review/issues.md`, C5 scans `<dir>/review/*.md` with the same stem→`<stem>-raw.*` evidence rule; both work identically in-flight and archived because the evidence travels with the dir. The `review/` entry itself is containment-checked (symlink/non-dir/escape blocks with a named defect).
- **`check` CK-10 re-rooted** — the secret tripwire sweeps every bundle's `review/` under `apriori/changes/` and `apriori/changes/archive/`, containment-guarded, symlinked entries warn-skipped by name.
- **Review-doc names drop their prefixes** — P5 evaluations are `spec-review-v{N}.md`, STEP5 consistency reviews are `step5-review-v{N}.md`, requirement reviews stay `req-review-v{N}.md`, all inside the bundle's `review/`.
- **`new` scaffolds the bundle skeleton** (`specs/`, `requirement/`, `review/`); `init` no longer creates a shared `apriori/review/`.
- **Node floor: ≥ 22** — `engines`, doctor's D1 check, and CI (22/24) move together.
- Both runbook editions and the concepts handbook are rewritten to bundle paths throughout; a corpus-level strip-scan test (PR-21) keeps the legacy roots from creeping back.

## 3.4.1 — 2026-07-12 · requirement paths carry their change

- **`requirement/<change>-req-v{N}.md` / `<change>-req-final.md` / `<change>-intent-card.md`** — requirement-stage paths gain the change prefix in both runbook editions, the concepts handbook, and `apriori new`'s scaffolded next-action, closing the global-path collision where parallel (or successive) changes overwrote each other's requirement history (dogfooded twice; ported from V1.4's stopgap). The root relocation (Change Bundle) remains scheduled for 4.0.
- **STEP6 preservation clause** — after the archive move and before the closeout commit, the change's requirement docs (all versions, plus the intent card if any) are copied into `apriori/changes/archive/<stamp>-<change>/requirement/`; the requirement history travels with its change.
- Already-archived changes keep their old file names (nothing parses requirement filenames). 2 new scenarios (PR-19, NW-05); 199 tests.

## 3.4.0 — 2026-07-12 · the second-review hardening release

Every item traces to the second external GPT-5.6 review (7 changes, each through the full V3 loop with adversarial codex reviews) plus one defect dogfooding found on ourselves.

- **The delta parser consumes its whole input** — `parseDeltaStrict` is a sequential line walker: misspelled section headings (`## ADDDED`), requirements/scenarios outside their legal home, and stray/duplicate/malformed CAS stamps are line-numbered problems instead of being silently re-homed into the wrong bucket; fences stay opaque; CRLF parses identically; well-formed deltas parse byte-identically (corpus-verified against every archived delta).
- **The TAP plan is a checked promise** — exactly one top-level plan allowed; plan-vs-parsed-points mismatch (truncated output) and duplicate test-point numbers are infra errors: `verify` exits 2, `gate` reports ERROR. Plan-less, skip-all (`1..0 # SKIP`), and node's nested TAP are unchanged.
- **`update` refreshes only what it can prove it owns** — `init` records what it creates in `apriori/managed.json` (exact-byte sha256); `update` refreshes manifest-listed, unmodified files only; modified/unmanaged/missing files are reported and left byte-identical. Pre-manifest projects are adopted on template-generation proof; manifest hygiene and realpath containment fail closed before any read.
- **The golden path resolves an explicit Git Bash on Windows** — `APRIORI_GIT_BASH` override, `where git` root derivation across the four Git-for-Windows layouts, conventional-install probes, a named cure when absent; the System32 WSL shim is never a candidate.
- **External side effects are a hard authorization rule** (runbook §1, both editions) — any operation mutating state outside the local repo/workspace needs the principal's explicit authorization (one-shot, or a named class/scope/expiry standing grant); gate consolidation never covers them; untrusted data may drive internal transitions but never authorizes crossing the boundary.
- **The issue ledger speaks a terminal-state vocabulary** — `verified` / `rejected-verified` (reviewer concurred, original reason preserved) / `waived` (human-only; gate C4 machine-checks the `gates:` entry by exact row ID) / `advisory-acked`. Unknown statuses block everywhere; archived changes must be all-terminal; STEP6 now requires a post-archive gate run in the gate-④ packet.
- **CAS enforcement is graded** — unstamped MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED deltas warn on every surface (`projection.unstampedMutations` in `--json`) and the new **gate C7** denies them by default, with visible waivers (`--no-cas` / a `| cas | optional |` config row). MODIFIED gains the trim-equality `unchanged` signature, so a stamped delta that already committed re-runs cleanly instead of dead-ending on its own stamp. Stamps become mandatory in 4.0.
- 21 new scenarios (AM-28..35, SR-26..32, GT-13..16, PR-17..18, UP-06..11, IN-13..17, GP-06..10); 197 tests.

Declared behavior changes (all fail-closed tightenings or additive): malformed delta structure now errors (was silently mis-bucketed); TAP plan mismatches now error (was GREEN-able); `update` now skips modified/unmanaged files (was clobbering); unknown ledger statuses now block gate C4; gate gains C7 (unstamped mutation deltas block by default — stamp them or waive visibly).

## 3.3.0 — 2026-07-11 · the productization release

- **Strict argument parsing everywhere** — every subcommand answers `--help`/`-h` (exit 0, wins over any other validation); unknown flags and stray positionals exit 2 naming themselves. A typo can no longer make a gate command act on the wrong target while exiting green. Three declared behavior changes: `apriori new a b` errors on `b` (was silently ignored), `apriori stamp --foo` is an unknown flag (was treated as the file), multi-value flags (`verify --specs`) stop consuming at any `-`-prefixed token (was `--`-only).
- **README split** — the README is now a ~117-line first screen with a machine-verified executable Quickstart; deep content moved to `docs/{concepts,legacy,ci,cli,troubleshooting}` (EN/CN pairs). `check --self` guards the new pairs (a one-sided pair fails) and resolves links per-file with cross-file fragment validation.
- **Golden path in CI** — the CI job packs the tarball (`npm pack`) and `scripts/golden-path.mjs` installs it into an isolated prefix and walks the README Quickstart verbatim on ubuntu and windows, asserting the documented exit sequence and final state. The published package is now proven as installed, not just as checked out.
- CHANGELOG.md, MIGRATING.md, SECURITY.md — the security fact-check drove a real hardening (doctor per-file flow-state containment).
- `examples/python-pytest/` — the any-language TAP pattern, verified in CI (ubuntu+windows, py3.12).
- CK-10 review-evidence secret tripwire (three airtight literal patterns) + provenance header convention + retention clause.
- `validation/` — the four labs' primary materials with an honest evidence-grade README (single-operator, no external replication yet).

## 3.2.0 — 2026-07-11 · the gate & doctor release

- **`apriori gate --change <name>`** — six mechanical checks, one exit code (0 PASS / 1 BLOCKED / 2 untrustworthy): stage-aware binding verify (projected in-flight, plain archived), tasks all checked, flow-state legality, ledger (open rows and reasonless rejections block), the verdict-evidence backstop (every VERDICT-bearing review doc needs its `S-raw.*` archive — the anti-simulated-review rule, now mechanical), git-based KB freshness that degrades to n/a rather than fabricating blocks. PASS covers the mechanical face only — human gates remain human, and the output says so.
- **`apriori doctor`** — seven onboarding checks with fix hints: Node floor, init scaffold (type-honest: files impersonating dirs are findings, never crashes), runbook freshness, tool pointers, a TAP plumbing probe with a complete failure taxonomy (test failures are explicitly NOT findings), store health, and a changes overview that surfaces pending gate-④ archived changes as information.
- 26 new scenarios (GT-01..12, DR-01..12, CL-09..10); 142 tests.

## 3.1.0 — 2026-07-10 · the projection release

- **`apriori verify --change <name>`** — verify against the in-memory projected (post-merge) store; the mid-change STEP5 gate. Fixes the verification-target misalignment: store-only scans miss new scenarios, dual-dir scans miscount MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED.
- **`apriori archive --change <name>`** — whole-change discovery, per-module dry-run by default, four-phase failure-atomic `--write` (preflight/stage/commit/move).
- **CAS base stamps + `apriori stamp <store-file>`** — opt-in divergence detection; a diverged store refuses to project or merge (the §4.11 serialize rule, mechanical).
- **Deprecated blocks excluded from ALL verify forms** — post-REMOVED stores no longer demand tests for removed behavior forever; lingering tests turn ORPHAN. (See MIGRATING.md.)
- Realpath containment on every participating path (incl. symlinked specs/, subdirs, the archive destination); strict delta hygiene (duplicate requirement names, malformed stamps, zero-op files — all fail closed).
- 26 new scenarios (SR-16..25, AM-13..27, CL-08); 116 tests.

## 3.0.1 — 2026-07-10 · fail-closed

- `verify` never GREEN on broken/vacuous runs: exit taxonomy 0/1/2; missing targets, zero scenarios, spawn/signal/bailout, zero-TAP output, and non-zero exits unexplained by TAP all refuse to pass. SKIP/TODO count as nothing; duplicate scenario IDs force GAPS; fenced scenarios are documentation; `XX-01b` never binds to `XX-01`.
- `check` split into consumer mode (default) vs `--self` (the apriori repo's own handbook checks) — consumers are never failed by our self-checks.
- `archive`: change-name validation + path-containment guard; transactional temp→move→rename commit; content-bearing zero-op deltas error instead of silently merging nothing.
- Cross-platform test fixtures (`node -e`, no printf/echo). Credit: external review by GPT-5.6.

## 3.0.0 — 2026-07-10 · first stable release

- Seven commands: `init` (6-tool scaffolding) / `new` / `status` / `verify` / `archive` / `check` / `update`, with `--json` and `--version`.
- Executable specs: scenario IDs bind to TAP test runs; `apriori verify` GREEN is a deterministic STEP5 gate.
- Native delta-spec archive merge (ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED, idempotent reruns, conflict stop).
- The full protocol: Brainstorm stance (two entry doors) → STEP0–6 state machine → heterogeneous adversarial review (issue ledger, verdict discipline) → living spec store + knowledge base with source-commit stamps; guarantee-claim discipline.
- Validation: three brainstorm-phase comparisons and three full builds vs OpenSpec & Superpowers, one legacy-project run; 15 alpha iterations. Single-operator validation — see the tag message for honest limits.

 succeeded in 142ms:
# Migrating

The 3.0.0 stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format, the flow-state schema and the `apriori/` layout only break in a major. Everything below is either additive or a declared fail-closed tightening.

## 3.4.0 → 3.4.1

Convention-only (no CLI behavior change): name requirement docs `requirement/<change>-req-v{N}.md` (finalized `<change>-req-final.md`; explore track `<change>-intent-card.md`) instead of the old global names, and at STEP6 copy them into the archived change dir per the runbook's new preservation clause. Existing archived changes keep their old names — nothing parses these filenames.

## 3.3.x → 3.4.0

Nothing on a documented success path changes. Five error-path behaviors are new — all fail-closed tightenings; each names its cure when it fires:

| situation | before | now |
|---|---|---|
| delta with a misspelled section heading / misplaced structure / stray stamp | content silently landed in the wrong bucket or vanished | line-numbered problem; `verify --change` exit 2, `archive` exit 1 |
| test output whose TAP plan doesn't match the parsed results (or duplicate test numbers, or two plans) | could verify GREEN | infra error: verify exit 2, gate ERROR |
| `apriori update` on a file it didn't create, or one you edited | overwritten with the template | reported (`unmanaged` / `modified`) and left byte-identical — delete + `apriori init --tools <t>` to hand a file back |
| ledger row with a status outside the vocabulary; archived change with non-terminal rows | gate C4 passed | C4 blocks naming the row (vocabulary: `open / fixed / rejected+reason / verified / rejected-verified / waived / advisory-acked`) |
| unstamped MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED delta at the gate | passed silently | **C7 blocks by default** — run `apriori stamp <store-file>`, or waive visibly (`gate --no-cas`, or a `| cas | optional |` process-config row). verify/archive only warn this minor; stamps become mandatory in 4.0 |

Additive: `apriori/managed.json` (written by init/update — track it in git), `projection.unstampedMutations` + `projection.notes` in `verify --change --json`, gate C7 in output/`--json`, the runbook's external-side-effect authorization rule and ledger vocabulary sections (both editions), a post-archive `gate` run required at STEP6, and a stamped delta that already fully committed now re-runs cleanly (the CAS rerun repair).

## 3.2.x → 3.3.0

**Strict argument parsing.** Nothing on a documented success path changes. Three error-path behaviors are new, all fail-closed:

| you typed | before | now |
|---|---|---|
| `apriori <sub> --typo-flag` | silently ignored (verify could fall back to the config and verify the WRONG spec set, green) | exit 2 naming the flag |
| `apriori new a b` | `b` silently ignored | exit 2 naming `b` |
| `apriori stamp --foo` | `--foo` treated as the store-file positional | exit 2, unknown flag |
| `apriori verify --specs a -x ...` | `-x` consumed as a spec path | exit 2 naming `-x` |

If a script of yours relied on silent-ignore, the exit-2 message names exactly what to remove. Every subcommand now answers `--help`.

**README/docs layout.** The handbook moved: concepts/workflow/example → `docs/concepts.md`, legacy guidance → `docs/legacy.md`, CI snippets → `docs/ci.md`, CLI reference + configuration → `docs/cli.md`, troubleshooting → `docs/troubleshooting.md` (all with `_cn` mirrors). Old deep links into README sections should point at the docs files.

## 3.0.x → 3.1.0

**Deprecated blocks stop being demanded (the one behavior change).** Before 3.1, a requirement archived via `## REMOVED Requirements` kept demanding its scenarios' tests forever (the deprecated block still carried `#### Scenario:` headings). From 3.1, every `verify` form excludes deprecated blocks; a test still tagged with a removed scenario's ID reports as ORPHAN. **Action:** delete tests for removed scenarios — ORPHAN is the reminder, not a bug.

**New, all opt-in / additive:** `verify --change` (use it as the mid-change STEP5 gate — the runbook was updated accordingly), `archive --change`, CAS base stamps (`apriori stamp`; unstamped deltas behave exactly as before), stricter delta hygiene (deltas that were silently collapsing — duplicate requirement names, malformed stamps — now error; they were corrupt input all along).

## 2.x / 1.x → 3.x

The 1.x/2.x lines used the OpenSpec adapter and live on their own branches (`main`, `v2`) — there is no in-place migration; 3.x is a reimplementation of the same artifact interface as a zero-dependency CLI. Start fresh with `apriori init` and copy your spec content into `apriori/specs/` (the Requirement/Scenario format is unchanged).

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori gate — aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code.
     4	 * 0 = PASS · 1 = BLOCKED · 2 = the evaluation itself is untrustworthy.
     5	 * Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human.
     6	 */
     7	const fs = require('fs');
     8	const path = require('path');
     9	const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
    10	const { parseFlowState, parseLedger } = require('./status');
    11	const { verify, configTestCmd } = require('./spec-runner');
    12	const { CHANGE_NAME_RE, containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    13	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    14	
    15	const STEP_ENUM = ['STEP0', 'STEP1', 'STEP2', 'STEP3', 'STEP4', 'STEP5', 'STEP6',
    16	  'INTENT-CARD', 'SPIKE', 'EXTRACTION', 'DONE', 'ABANDONED'];
    17	const TIER_ENUM = ['trivial', 'medium', 'large'];
    18	const CAVEAT = 'mechanical checks only; human gates remain human';
    19	
    20	function err(res, msg) { res.errors.push(msg); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
    21	
    22	// stage resolution: in-flight first, else newest archived (exact stamp regex, lexicographic last)
    23	function resolveChange(cwd, name) {
    24	  const changesDir = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
    25	  const inflight = path.join(changesDir, name);
    26	  if (fs.existsSync(inflight)) {
    27	    if (!containsReal(changesDir, inflight)) return { error: `change path escapes ${changesDir}` };
    28	    return { stage: 'in-flight', dir: inflight };
    29	  }
    30	  const archRoot = path.join(changesDir, 'archive');
    31	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
    32	    const re = new RegExp(`^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}T\\d{4}-${name}$`);
    33	    // candidates must be DIRECTORIES (following symlinks) — a stray file with a stamp name is ignored
    34	    const hits = fs.readdirSync(archRoot).filter((b) => {
    35	      if (!re.test(b)) return false;
    36	      try { return fs.statSync(path.join(archRoot, b)).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; }
    37	    }).sort();
    38	    if (hits.length) {
    39	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, hits[hits.length - 1]);
    40	      // containment against the ARCHIVE root — a symlink pointing elsewhere under changes/ is still an escape
    41	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) return { error: `archived change path escapes ${archRoot}` };
    42	      return { stage: 'archived', dir };
    43	    }
    44	  }
    45	  return { error: `change '${name}' found neither at ${inflight} nor under ${archRoot}` };
    46	}
    47	
    48	// --- individual checks; each returns {id, status: 'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a', detail} ---
    49	
    50	function checkFlowState(state, name) {
    51	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'lineage', 'current-step']) {
    52	    const v = state[key];
    53	    if (v === undefined || v === '') return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: required key '${key}' missing` };
    54	    if (v.includes('<') || v.includes('>')) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: '${key}' is an unfilled placeholder (${v})` };
    55	  }
    56	  if (state.change !== name) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'change' is '${state.change}', expected '${name}'` };
    57	  if (!STEP_ENUM.includes(state['current-step'])) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'current-step' '${state['current-step']}' not in the legal vocabulary` };
    58	  if (!TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier)) return { id: 'C3', status: 'blocked', detail: `flow-state: 'tier' '${state.tier}' not in {trivial, medium, large}` };
    59	  return { id: 'C3', status: 'pass', detail: `legal (tier ${state.tier}, ${state['current-step']})` };
    60	}
    61	
    62	function checkTasks(dir, tier) {
    63	  const p = path.join(dir, 'tasks.md');
    64	  if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    65	    return tier === 'trivial'
    66	      ? { id: 'C2', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no tasks.md — trivial tier has no STEP2' }
    67	      : { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md missing at ${p}` };
    68	  }
    69	  const open = (fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8').match(/^\s*-\s\[\s\]/gm) || []).length;
    70	  return open
    71	    ? { id: 'C2', status: 'blocked', detail: `tasks.md has ${open} unchecked box(es)` }
    72	    : { id: 'C2', status: 'pass', detail: 'all tasks checked' };
    73	}

    75	// the ledger vocabulary, one classifier shared by C4 and the corpus test (GT-15).
    76	// leading token, case-insensitive; rejected-verified BEFORE rejected (alternation order).
    77	const STATUS_RE = /^(open|fixed|verified|rejected-verified|rejected|advisory-acked|waived)\b/i;
    78	function classifyStatus(status) {
    79	  const m = STATUS_RE.exec(status);
    80	  if (!m) return { legal: false, terminal: false, needsReason: false, hasReason: false, isWaived: false };
   250	  if (noCas) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (--no-cas)' };
   251	  if (configCas(cwd) === 'optional') return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'waived (process-config cas: optional)' };
   252	  const um = (projection && projection.unstampedMutations) || [];
   253	  if (!um.length) return { id: 'C7', status: 'pass', detail: 'every mutation delta is stamped' };
   254	  return { id: 'C7', status: 'blocked',
   255	    detail: `unstamped mutation delta(s): ${um.join(', ')} — run: apriori stamp <store-file> (or waive with --no-cas / a process-config cas: optional row)` };
   256	}

   258	// The whole evaluation. → { code, stage, checks, result, blocked, errors }
   259	function runGate(opts) {
   260	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
   261	  const res = { code: 0, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'PASS', blocked: 0, errors: [], change: opts.change || null };
   262	  if (!opts.change) return err(res, 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]');
   263	  if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(opts.change)) return err(res, `invalid change name '${opts.change}' — bare kebab-case only`);
   264	  const loc = resolveChange(cwd, opts.change);
   265	  if (loc.error) return err(res, loc.error);
   266	  res.stage = loc.stage;
   267	  const flowPath = path.join(loc.dir, 'flow-state.md');
   268	  if (!fs.existsSync(flowPath)) return err(res, `no readable flow-state.md at ${flowPath} — tier-aware checks are impossible`);
   269	  let state;
   270	  try { state = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(flowPath, 'utf8')); }
   271	  catch (e) { return err(res, `flow-state unreadable: ${e.message}`); }
   272	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || configTestCmd(cwd);
   273	  if (!testCmd) return err(res, 'no test command: pass --test-cmd or add a test-cmd row to apriori/process-config.md');
   274	
   275	  const b = checkBinding(cwd, opts.change, loc.stage, testCmd);
   276	  if (b.infra) { for (const e of b.infra) res.errors.push(`verify: ${e}`); res.result = 'ERROR'; res.code = 2; return res; }
   277	  res.checks.push(b.check);
   278	  const tier = TIER_ENUM.includes(state.tier) ? state.tier : null;
   279	  res.checks.push(checkTasks(loc.dir, tier));
   280	  res.checks.push(checkFlowState(state, opts.change));
   281	  const rdDefect = reviewDirDefect(loc.dir);
   282	  if (rdDefect) {
   283	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C4', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   284	    res.checks.push({ id: 'C5', status: 'blocked', detail: rdDefect });
   285	  } else {
   286	    res.checks.push(checkLedger(tier, loc.stage, loc.dir));
   287	    res.checks.push(checkEvidence(loc.dir));
   288	  }
   289	
   290	  res.checks.push(checkKb(cwd, loc.dir));
   291	  res.checks.push(checkCas(b.projection, loc.stage, !!opts.noCas, cwd));
   292	
   293	  res.blocked = res.checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'blocked').length;
   294	  res.result = res.blocked ? 'BLOCKED' : 'PASS';
   295	  res.code = res.blocked ? 1 : 0;
   296	  return res;
   297	}
   298	
   299	function toJson(res) {
   300	  return { change: res.change, stage: res.stage, checks: res.checks, result: res.result, blocked: res.blocked, errors: res.errors };
   301	}
   302	
   303	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori gate --change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]';
   304	
   305	function cli(argv) {
   306	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'gate', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   307	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--test-cmd': 'value', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag', '--no-cas': 'flag' },
   308	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ change: null, stage: null, checks: [], result: 'ERROR', blocked: 0, errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   309	    const a = { change: f['--change'] || null, testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null, cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'], noCas: !!f['--no-cas'] };
   310	    const res = runGate(a);
   311	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   312	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('gate: ' + e);
   313	    const mark = { pass: '✓', blocked: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   314	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.status === 'blocked' ? 'BLOCKED — ' : ''}${c.detail}`);
   315	    if (res.code !== 2) console.log(res.code === 0 ? `\nGATE: PASS — ${CAVEAT}` : `\nGATE: BLOCKED (${res.blocked} item(s))`);
   316	    return res.code;
   317	  });
   318	}

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori status — answer "where am I / what's left" for a change.
     4	 * Reads the flow-state file + the issue ledger; zero deps, pure Node stdlib.
     5	 */
     6	const fs = require('fs');
     7	const path = require('path');
     8	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
     9	
    10	// parse `key: value` lines from a flow-state file → object; plus the last gate line
    11	function parseFlowState(text) {
    12	  const out = {};
    13	  for (const key of ['change', 'tier', 'track', 'current-step', 'round', 'next-action', 'lineage']) {
    14	    const m = text.match(new RegExp('^' + key + ':\\s*(.+)$', 'm'));
    15	    if (m) out[key] = m[1].replace(/\s*#.*$/, '').trim();
    16	  }
    17	  const gates = [...text.matchAll(/^\s*-\s*(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\S*\s+.+)$/gm)].map((m) => m[1].trim());
    18	  out.lastGate = gates.length ? gates[gates.length - 1] : null;
    19	  return out;
    20	}
    21	
    22	// parse a P0 ledger markdown table → rows with {id, status}
    23	function parseLedger(text) {
    24	  const rows = [];
    25	  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    26	    const m = line.match(/^\|\s*([^|]+?)\s*\|.*\|\s*([^|]+?)\s*\|\s*$/);
    27	    if (!m) continue;
    28	    const id = m[1].trim(), status = m[2].trim();
    29	    if (id === 'ID' || /^-+$/.test(id)) continue;         // header / separator
    30	    rows.push({ id, status });
    31	  }
    32	  return rows;
    33	}
    34	
    35	function activeChanges(root) {
    36	  const dir = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes');
    37	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];
    38	  return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })
    39	    .filter((e) => e.isDirectory() && e.name !== 'archive')
    40	    .map((e) => e.name).sort();
    41	}
    42	
    43	function changeStatus(root, change) {
    44	  const fsPath = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', change, 'flow-state.md');
    45	  const state = fs.existsSync(fsPath) ? parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fsPath, 'utf8')) : null;
    46	  const ledgerPath = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', change, 'review', 'issues.md');
    47	  const rows = fs.existsSync(ledgerPath) ? parseLedger(fs.readFileSync(ledgerPath, 'utf8')) : [];
    48	  const open = rows.filter((r) => /^open\b/i.test(r.status));
    49	  return { change, state, open, hasFlowState: !!state };
    50	}
    51	
    52	function formatOne(s) {
    53	  const lines = [];
    54	  if (!s.state) { lines.push(`change: ${s.change} (no flow-state file found)`); }
    55	  else {
    56	    lines.push(`change:       ${s.state.change || s.change}`);
    57	    lines.push(`step:         ${s.state['current-step'] || '?'}   (tier ${s.state.tier || '?'}, track ${s.state.track || '?'})`);
    58	    if (s.state['next-action']) lines.push(`next-action:  ${s.state['next-action']}`);
    59	    if (s.state.lastGate) lines.push(`last gate:    ${s.state.lastGate}`);
    60	  }
    61	  lines.push(`open ledger:  ${s.open.length}` + (s.open.length ? ` — ${s.open.map((r) => r.id).join(', ')}` : ''));
    62	  return lines.join('\n');
    63	}
    64	
    65	// machine-consumable shape for one change
    66	function toJson(s) {
    67	  return {
    68	    change: s.change,
    69	    step: s.state ? s.state['current-step'] || null : null,
    70	    tier: s.state ? s.state.tier || null : null,
    71	    track: s.state ? s.state.track || null : null,
    72	    lineage: s.state ? s.state.lineage || null : null,
    73	    nextAction: s.state ? s.state['next-action'] || null : null,
    74	    lastGate: s.state ? s.state.lastGate : null,
    75	    hasFlowState: s.hasFlowState,
    76	    openLedger: s.open.map((r) => r.id),
    77	  };
    78	}
    79	
    80	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori status [--change <name>] [--json]';
    81	
    82	function cli(argv) {
    83	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'status', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
    84	    flags: { '--change': 'value', '--json': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
    85	    const root = process.cwd();
    86	    const change = f['--change'] || null, json = !!f['--json'];
    87	    if (change) {
    88	      const s = changeStatus(root, change);
    89	      console.log(json ? JSON.stringify(toJson(s), null, 2) : formatOne(s));
    90	      return 0;
    91	    }
    92	    const changes = activeChanges(root);
    93	    if (json) {
    94	      console.log(JSON.stringify({ changes: changes.map((c) => toJson(changeStatus(root, c))) }, null, 2));
    95	      return 0;
    96	    }
    97	    if (!changes.length) { console.log('No active changes under apriori/changes/.'); return 0; }
    98	    console.log(`Active changes (${changes.length}):\n`);
    99	    for (const c of changes) {
   100	      const s = changeStatus(root, c);
   101	      const step = s.state ? s.state['current-step'] || '?' : 'no flow-state';
   102	      console.log(`  ${c}  —  ${step}, ${s.open.length} open`);
   103	    }
   104	    console.log('\nRun `apriori status --change <name>` for detail.');
   105	    return 0;
   106	  });
   107	}
   108	
   109	module.exports = { parseFlowState, parseLedger, activeChanges, changeStatus, formatOne, toJson, cli };

   254	
   255	function renderStore(storeText, store) {
   256	  const idx = storeText.indexOf('### Requirement:');
   257	  const header = idx >= 0 ? storeText.slice(0, idx) : '';
   258	  return header + [...store.values()].join('\n');
   259	}
   260	
   261	// ---- paths and discovery ---------------------------------------------------
   262	
   263	// Realpath containment: target (followed through symlinks; not-yet-existing targets judged
   264	// by their nearest existing ancestor) must resolve STRICTLY inside root. Fail-closed.
   265	function containsReal(root, target) {
   266	  let realRoot;
   267	  try { realRoot = fs.realpathSync(root); } catch { return false; }
   268	  let cur = path.resolve(target);
   269	  const rest = [];
   270	  while (!fs.existsSync(cur)) {
   271	    const parent = path.dirname(cur);
   272	    if (parent === cur) return false;
   273	    rest.unshift(path.basename(cur));
   274	    cur = parent;
   275	  }
   276	  let realCur;
   277	  try { realCur = fs.realpathSync(cur); } catch { return false; }
   278	  const full = rest.length ? path.join(realCur, ...rest) : realCur;
   279	  return full !== realRoot && (full + path.sep).startsWith(realRoot + path.sep);
   280	}
   281	
   282	function mdFilesUnder(dir) {
   283	  const out = [];
   284	  const walk = (d) => {
   285	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   286	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   287	      // Dirents reflect lstat: a symlink is neither file nor directory. Follow it explicitly —
   288	      // silently skipping a symlinked dir would hide deltas (fail-open); containment judges the target.
   289	      let isDir = e.isDirectory();
   290	      if (e.isSymbolicLink()) {
   291	        try { isDir = fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { isDir = false; /* broken link: fall through, read fails loudly */ }
   292	      }
   293	      if (isDir) walk(p);
   294	      else if (e.name.endsWith('.md')) out.push(p);
   295	    }
   296	  };
   297	  if (fs.existsSync(dir)) walk(dir);
   298	  return out.sort();
   299	}
   300	
   301	// Discover a change's delta files. → { files: [{abs, suffix}], errors: [] } — errors are
   302	// VALIDATION class (exit 2 on both surfaces): bad name, missing dir, escape, zero files.
   303	function discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name) {
   304	  const errors = [];
   305	  if (!name || !CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(name)) return { files: [], errors: [`invalid change name '${name}' — bare kebab-case only`] };
   306	  const changeDir = path.join(changesDirAbs, name);
   307	  if (!fs.existsSync(changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change '${name}' not found under ${changesDirAbs}`] };
   308	  if (!containsReal(changesDirAbs, changeDir)) return { files: [], errors: [`change path escapes ${changesDirAbs}: ${changeDir}`] };
   309	  const specsDir = path.join(changeDir, 'specs');
   310	  // a symlinked specs/ pointing outside would smuggle its target in as the containment ROOT —
   311	  // require specs/ itself to really live inside the change dir before trusting it as a root
   312	  if (fs.existsSync(specsDir) && !containsReal(changeDir, specsDir))
   313	    return { files: [], errors: [`specs dir escapes the change dir (symlink?): ${specsDir}`] };
   314	  const all = mdFilesUnder(specsDir);
   315	  if (!all.length) return { files: [], errors: [`no delta spec files (*.md) found under ${specsDir}`] };
   316	  const files = [];
   317	  for (const abs of all) {
   318	    if (!containsReal(specsDir, abs)) { errors.push(`delta path escapes the change root: ${abs}`); continue; }
   319	    files.push({ abs, suffix: path.relative(specsDir, abs).split(path.sep).join('/') });
   320	  }
   321	  return { files, errors };
   322	}
   323	
   324	// Build the projection: apply every delta to its mapped store file IN MEMORY.
   325	// → { texts: Map<suffix, projectedText>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches,
   326	//     hygiene, validation } — texts covers ALL store files (pass-through + projected).
   327	function buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change) {
   328	  const texts = new Map(), perModule = new Map(), modules = [];
   329	  const conflicts = [], casMismatches = [], hygiene = [], validation = [];
   330	  const unstampedMutations = [], notes = [], repaired = [];
   331	  for (const abs of mdFilesUnder(storeRoot))
   332	    texts.set(path.relative(storeRoot, abs).split(path.sep).join('/'), fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8'));
   333	  for (const { abs, suffix } of deltaFiles) {
   334	    modules.push(suffix);
   335	    const target = path.join(storeRoot, ...suffix.split('/'));
   336	    if (!containsReal(storeRoot, target)) { validation.push(`store target escapes ${storeRoot}: ${target}`); continue; }
   337	    const raw = fs.readFileSync(abs, 'utf8');
   338	    if (!raw.trim()) { hygiene.push(`empty delta file: ${abs}`); continue; }
   339	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(raw);
   340	    if (problems.length) { hygiene.push(...problems.map((p) => `${abs}: ${p}`)); continue; }
   341	    if (deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) { hygiene.push(`parsed 0 delta operations from ${abs} — it has content but no recognized operations`); continue; }
   342	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) unstampedMutations.push(suffix);   // WARN grade (callers print)
   343	    const storeText = texts.get(suffix) || '';
   344	    if (stamp) {
   345	      const actual = texts.has(suffix) ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   346	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   347	        // rerun repair: a mismatch whose every op is already applied is the resumed-rerun
   348	        // signature — divergence with ANY real pending op still fails exactly as before
   349	        const probe = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   350	        const pending = probe.merged.length + probe.modified.length + probe.deprecated.length + probe.renamed.length;
   351	        if (pending === 0 && probe.conflicts.length === 0) {
   352	          notes.push(`${suffix}: stamp mismatch but the delta is already fully applied — rerun accepted`);
   353	          perModule.set(suffix, probe);
   354	          repaired.push(suffix);
   355	          continue;                                          // contributes no writes
   356	        }
   357	        casMismatches.push(`base mismatch for ${target}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual}`); continue;
   358	      }
   359	    }
   360	    const r = merge(storeText, delta, change);
   361	    perModule.set(suffix, r);
   362	    if (r.conflicts.length) { conflicts.push(...r.conflicts.map((c) => `${suffix}: ${c}`)); continue; }
   363	    texts.set(suffix, renderStore(storeText, r.store));
   364	  }
   365	  modules.sort();
   366	  return { texts, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired };
   367	}
   368	
   369	// ---- archive commit machinery ----------------------------------------------
   370	
   371	// colon-free date-time stamp YYYY-MM-DDThhmm (CLI's own clock; never an agent guess)
   372	function archiveStamp(d) {
   373	  const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
   374	  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}T${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}`;
   375	}
   500	    catch (e) { console.error(`stamp: cannot read ${p}: ${e.message}`); return 2; }
   501	    console.log(`<!-- apriori-base: ${fingerprint(text)} -->`);
   502	    return 0;
   503	  });
   504	}

   506	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]\n   or: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]';
   507	
   508	function cli(argv) {
   509	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'archive', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   510	    flags: { '--store': 'value', '--delta': 'value', '--change': 'value', '--write': 'flag', '--changes-dir': 'value' } }, (f) => {
   511	    const a = { store: f['--store'] || null, delta: f['--delta'] || null, change: f['--change'] || null,
   512	      write: !!f['--write'], changesDir: f['--changes-dir'] || null };
   513	  // high-level form: --change alone (no --store/--delta) drives the whole change
   514	    if (a.change && !a.store && !a.delta) {
   515	      const r = archiveChange({ cwd: process.cwd(), change: a.change, write: a.write,
   516	        changesDir: a.changesDir || undefined, changesDirExplicit: !!a.changesDir });
   517	      for (const l of r.out) console.log(l);
   518	      for (const l of r.err) console.error(l);
   519	      return r.code;
   520	    }
   521	    if (a.change && (a.store || a.delta) && !(a.store && a.delta)) {
   522	      console.error('usage: apriori archive --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (high-level form)\n   or: apriori archive --store <f> --delta <f> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]  (single-file form)\n(--change cannot be combined with only one of --store/--delta)');
   523	      return 2;
   524	    }
   525	    if (!a.store || !a.delta || !a.change) { console.error(USAGE); return 2; }
   526	    if (!CHANGE_NAME_RE.test(a.change)) { console.error(`archive: invalid change name '${a.change}' — bare kebab-case only (path segments are not allowed)`); return 2; }
   527	    if (a.changesDir && !fs.existsSync(path.join(a.changesDir, a.change))) {
   528	      console.error(`archive: change '${a.change}' not found under ${a.changesDir} — nothing to move`); return 2;
   529	    }
   530	    // first archive in a repo: the store file may not exist yet — start from an empty store
   531	    const storeExists = fs.existsSync(a.store);
   532	    const storeText = storeExists ? fs.readFileSync(a.store, 'utf8') : '';
   533	    if (!storeExists) console.log(`store ${a.store} does not exist yet — will be created${a.write ? '' : ' on --write'}`);
   534	    const deltaText = fs.readFileSync(a.delta, 'utf8');
   535	    const { delta, stamp, problems } = parseDeltaStrict(deltaText);
   536	    if (problems.length) {
   537	      for (const p of problems) console.error(`archive: ${a.delta}: ${p}`);
   538	      console.log('\nRESULT: MALFORMED DELTA — nothing written');
   539	      return 1;
   540	    }
   541	    // Guard against a silent no-op: a delta with real content that parsed to zero operations
   542	    // is almost always a format mismatch (wrong heading level/keyword), not an empty change.
   543	    if (deltaText.trim() && deltaOpCount(delta) === 0) {
   544	      console.error(`archive: parsed 0 delta operations from ${a.delta} — it has content but no recognized operations.`);
   545	      console.error('  expected `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED Requirements` (h2) with `### Requirement: <name>` (h3) blocks,');
   546	      console.error('  or `## RENAMED Requirements` with `- Old -> New` lines. Nothing written.');
   547	      return 1;
   548	    }
   549	    // WARN grade (AM-32): mutation ops without a stamp merge, but never silently
   550	    if (!stamp && mutationOpCount(delta) > 0) console.log(`warning: ${unstampedWarning(a.delta)}`);
   551	    // CAS: a present base stamp must match the store the delta is about to merge into (AM-24/AM-26)
   552	    if (stamp) {
   553	      const actual = storeExists ? fingerprint(storeText) : 'new';
   554	      if (stamp !== actual) {
   555	        console.error(`archive: base mismatch for ${a.store}: delta expects ${stamp}, store is ${actual} — nothing written (serialize per §4.11)`);
   556	        return 1;
   557	      }
   558	    }
   559	    const { store, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged } = merge(storeText, delta, a.change);
   560	
   561	    for (const [label, ids] of [['merged (ADDED)', merged], ['modified (MODIFIED)', modified], ['deprecated (REMOVED)', deprecated], ['renamed (RENAMED)', renamed], ['already merged (no-op)', unchanged]])
   562	      if (ids.length) console.log(`${label}: ${ids.join(', ')}`);
   563	
   564	    if (conflicts.length) {
   565	      console.log('\nCONFLICTS (stop — open a ledger issue, human resolves):');
   566	      for (const c of conflicts) console.log(`  ✗ ${c}`);
   567	      console.log('\nRESULT: CONFLICT — nothing written');
   568	      return 1;
   569	    }
   570	    if (a.write) {
   571	      // Transactional ordering: stage the store to a temp file, do the (atomic) dir move, then
   572	      // commit the store via rename. If the move fails, the temp is discarded and the store on
   573	      // disk stays byte-for-byte untouched — never a half-completed archive.
   574	      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(a.store), { recursive: true });
   575	      const tmp = a.store + '.tmp-archive';
   576	      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, renderStore(storeText, store));
   577	      let moved = null;
   578	      if (a.changesDir) {
   579	        try { moved = archiveChangeDir(a.changesDir, a.change, new Date()); }
   580	        catch (e) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: ${e.message} — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   581	        if (moved === null) { fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true }); console.error(`archive: change dir vanished mid-run — store left untouched`); return 1; }
   582	      }
   583	      fs.renameSync(tmp, a.store);
   584	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED — ${a.store} rewritten${moved ? `; change archived → ${moved}` : ''}`);
   585	    } else {
   586	      console.log(`\nRESULT: MERGED (dry-run; ${store.size} requirements in result) — pass --write to apply`);
   587	    }
   588	    return 0;
   589	  });
   590	}

     9	function project(changes) {
    10	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-st-'));
    11	  for (const [name, { flow, ledger }] of Object.entries(changes)) {
    12	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name), { recursive: true });
    13	    if (flow) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'flow-state.md'), flow);
    14	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'review'), { recursive: true });
    15	    if (ledger) { fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'review'), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'review', 'issues.md'), ledger); }
    16	  }
    17	  return root;
    18	}

    26	  - 2026-07-07T02:00 gate③: approved
    27	`;

    31	| D-2 | y | low | 1 | fixed |
    32	| D-3 | z | med | 2 | open |
    33	| D-4 | w | low | 2 | advisory-acked |
    34	`;

    39	  assert.strictEqual(s.state['current-step'], 'STEP2');
    40	  assert.match(s.state['next-action'], /spawn P5 reviewer/);
    41	  assert.deepStrictEqual(s.open.map((r) => r.id), ['D-1', 'D-3']);

    44	  assert.match(out, /next-action:.*spawn P5 reviewer/);
    45	  assert.match(out, /last gate:.*gate③: approved/);          // last gate surfaced
    46	  assert.match(out, /open ledger:  2 — D-1, D-3/);
    47	});

    57	  finally { console.log = log; process.chdir(cwd); }

     1	# Changelog
     2	
     3	All notable changes to `apriori-cli`. Versions follow semver; the stability promise: CLI surface & flags, `--json` shapes, the delta format and the flow-state schema only break in a major.
     4	
     5	## 4.0.0 — 2026-07-12 · the change bundle
     6	
     7	Everything a change produces lives in ONE directory: `apriori/changes/<name>/` — `flow-state.md`, the `requirement/` history (plain names: `req-v{N}.md`, `req-final.md`, `intent-card.md`), `gap-report.md`, `proposal.md`, `design.md`, `tasks.md`, the `specs/` deltas, the `review/` evidence (ledger `issues.md`, review docs, raws), and `spike/` on the explore track. The five scattered per-change roots (`requirement/`, `spike/`, `apriori/review/`, `apriori/design/`, `apriori/explore/`) cease to exist.
     8	
     9	- **The archive move carries the whole bundle** — `apriori archive` moves the change dir in one atomic rename with everything already inside; the 3.4.x staging phase is deleted. Explore-track `spike/` is deleted or quarantined by the executor *before* the archive action (the command never touches it).
    10	- **Gates read the bundle** — C4's ledger lives at `<dir>/review/issues.md`, C5 scans `<dir>/review/*.md` with the same stem→`<stem>-raw.*` evidence rule; both work identically in-flight and archived because the evidence travels with the dir. The `review/` entry itself is containment-checked (symlink/non-dir/escape blocks with a named defect).
    11	- **`check` CK-10 re-rooted** — the secret tripwire sweeps every bundle's `review/` under `apriori/changes/` and `apriori/changes/archive/`, containment-guarded, symlinked entries warn-skipped by name.
    12	- **Review-doc names drop their prefixes** — P5 evaluations are `spec-review-v{N}.md`, STEP5 consistency reviews are `step5-review-v{N}.md`, requirement reviews stay `req-review-v{N}.md`, all inside the bundle's `review/`.
    13	- **`new` scaffolds the bundle skeleton** (`specs/`, `requirement/`, `review/`); `init` no longer creates a shared `apriori/review/`.
    14	- **Node floor: ≥ 22** — `engines`, doctor's D1 check, and CI (22/24) move together.
    15	- Both runbook editions and the concepts handbook are rewritten to bundle paths throughout; a corpus-level strip-scan test (PR-21) keeps the legacy roots from creeping back.
    16	
    17	## 3.4.1 — 2026-07-12 · requirement paths carry their change

 succeeded in 139ms:
     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori update — refresh tool-owned scaffolded files after a CLI upgrade.
     4	 * Tool-owned: apriori/runbook.md (copied from the package's own RUNBOOK.md — single source)
     5	 * and per-tool command files that already exist (from templates/command.md).
     6	 * User-owned is NEVER touched: process-config.md, specs/, changes/, review/, truth/,
     7	 * and the rules files the init pointer was appended to (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, …).
     8	 * Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     9	 */
    10	const fs = require('fs');
    11	const path = require('path');
    12	const { TOOLS } = require('./init');
    13	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    14	const managed = require('./managed');
    15	
    16	const PKG_ROOT = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    17	const RUNBOOK_SRC = path.join(PKG_ROOT, 'RUNBOOK.md');
    18	const COMMAND_SRC = path.join(PKG_ROOT, 'templates', 'command.md');
    19	
    20	// Refresh one existing target from src; returns 'updated' | 'up-to-date'.
    21	function refresh(target, src, dryRun) {
    22	  const want = fs.readFileSync(src, 'utf8');
    23	  if (fs.readFileSync(target, 'utf8') === want) return 'up-to-date';
    24	  if (!dryRun) fs.writeFileSync(target, want);
    25	  return 'updated';
    26	}
    27	
    28	const CURE = "delete it and rerun 'apriori init --tools <t>' to hand it back to the tool";
    29	
    30	// Refresh tool-owned files under root — but only those apriori/managed.json proves the
    31	// tool owns AND the user hasn't modified. Everything else is reported and left alone.
    32	// Pre-manifest projects are adopted on proof (runbook unconditionally; command files
    33	// only when their bytes match a shipped template generation).
    34	// Returns { actions: [{file, action}] }; throws on no runbook or manifest hygiene errors.
    35	function run(root, opts = {}) {
    36	  const rb = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md');
    37	  if (!fs.existsSync(rb)) throw new Error(`no apriori/runbook.md here — run 'apriori init' first`);
    38	  const manifest = managed.readManifest(root, TOOLS);       // hygiene throws before any touch
    39	  const generations = opts.generations || managed.TEMPLATE_GENERATIONS;
    40	  const dry = !!opts.dryRun;
    41	  const actions = [];
    42	  const files = manifest ? { ...manifest.files } : {};
    43	  let manifestDirty = manifest === null;                    // adoption always materializes it
    44	
    45	  // one candidate: managed semantics when listed; adoption when pre-manifest; unmanaged otherwise
    46	  const consider = (rel, srcAbs, adoptable) => {
    47	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    48	    const listed = manifest && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(manifest.files, rel);
    49	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    50	      if (listed) actions.push({ file: rel, action: "missing (skipped — recreating is init's job)" });
    51	      return;
    52	    }
    53	    managed.assertContained(root, rel);                     // before ANY read or hash
    54	    const cur = managed.hashFile(p);
    55	    if (listed) {
    56	      if (cur !== manifest.files[rel]) { actions.push({ file: rel, action: `modified (skipped — locally modified; ${CURE})` }); return; }
    57	      const act = refresh(p, srcAbs, dry);
    58	      actions.push({ file: rel, action: act });
    59	      if (act === 'updated') { files[rel] = managed.hashBytes(fs.readFileSync(srcAbs)); manifestDirty = true; }
    60	      return;
    61	    }
    62	    if (manifest === null && adoptable(cur)) {              // pre-manifest adoption on proof
    63	      const act = refresh(p, srcAbs, dry);
    64	      actions.push({ file: rel, action: act });
    65	      files[rel] = act === 'updated' ? managed.hashBytes(fs.readFileSync(srcAbs)) : cur;
    66	      return;
    67	    }
    68	    actions.push({ file: rel, action: `unmanaged (skipped — not created by this tool; ${CURE})` });
    69	  };
    70	
    71	  // runbook: adopted unconditionally pre-manifest (its refresh is update's reason to exist)
    72	  consider('apriori/runbook.md', opts.runbookSrc || RUNBOOK_SRC, () => true);
    73	  // protocol-required scaffolding the refreshed runbook relies on (IN-11): create if missing, NEVER modify
    74	  const gi = path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore');
    75	  if (!fs.existsSync(gi)) {
    76	    if (!dry) { fs.writeFileSync(gi, 'tmp/\n'); fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'tmp'), { recursive: true }); }
    77	    actions.push({ file: 'apriori/.gitignore', action: 'created' });
    78	  }
    79	  for (const key of Object.keys(TOOLS)) {
    80	    const rel = TOOLS[key].command;
    81	    if (!rel) continue;
    82	    consider(rel, opts.commandSrc || COMMAND_SRC, (cur) => generations.includes(cur));
    83	  }
    84	  if (manifestDirty && !dry) managed.writeManifest(root, files);
    85	  return { actions };
    86	}
    87	
    88	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori update [--dry-run]';
    89	
    90	function cli(argv) {
    91	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'update', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0, flags: { '--dry-run': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
    92	    const dryRun = !!f['--dry-run'];
    93	    let res;
    94	    try { res = run(process.cwd(), { dryRun }); }
    95	    catch (e) { console.error('  ' + e.message); return 1; }
    96	    for (const { file, action } of res.actions)
    97	      console.log(`  ${action === 'updated' ? '✓' : '·'} ${file}  (${action})`);
    98	    const n = res.actions.filter((a) => a.action === 'updated').length;
    99	    const v = require('../package.json').version;
   100	    console.log(n
   101	      ? `\n  ${n} file(s) ${dryRun ? 'would be ' : ''}refreshed to apriori-cli ${v}.`
   102	      : `\n  everything already matches apriori-cli ${v}.`);
   103	    console.log('  (user-owned files — process-config.md, specs/, changes/, rules files — are never touched.)');
   104	    return 0;
   105	  });
   106	}
   107	
   108	module.exports = { run, refresh, cli };

    16	const sha = (buf) => 'sha256:' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buf).digest('hex');
    17	const shaFile = (p) => sha(fs.readFileSync(p));
    18	function writeManifest(root, files) {
    19	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'managed.json'),
    20	    JSON.stringify({ version: 1, files }, null, 2) + '\n');

    23	// scaffold a claude-configured project, then age some tool-owned files.
    24	// The manifest records the AGED bytes — exactly what an older CLI would have
    25	// written and recorded — so update sees tool-owned-and-unmodified files.
    26	function agedProject() {
    27	  const root = tmp();
    28	  init.scaffold(root, ['claude']);
    29	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md'), '# old runbook from a previous CLI version\n');
    30	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, '.claude', 'commands', 'apriori.md'), 'old command body\n');
    31	  writeManifest(root, {
    32	    'apriori/runbook.md': shaFile(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md')),
    33	    '.claude/commands/apriori.md': shaFile(path.join(root, '.claude', 'commands', 'apriori.md')),
    34	  });
    35	  return root;
    36	}
    37	
    38	test('UP-01 refreshes the runbook copy and existing command files', () => {
    39	  const root = agedProject();
    40	  const { actions } = update.run(root);
    41	  const byFile = Object.fromEntries(actions.map((a) => [a.file, a.action]));
    42	  assert.strictEqual(byFile['apriori/runbook.md'], 'updated');
    43	  assert.strictEqual(byFile['.claude/commands/apriori.md'], 'updated');
    44	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md'), 'utf8'), PKG_RUNBOOK);
    45	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, '.claude', 'commands', 'apriori.md'), 'utf8'), PKG_COMMAND);
    46	  // second run: everything up-to-date
    47	  for (const a of update.run(root).actions) assert.strictEqual(a.action, 'up-to-date');
    48	});
    49	
    50	test('UP-02 user-owned files are never touched, nothing new is created', () => {
    51	  const root = agedProject();
    52	  const cfgPath = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'process-config.md');
    53	  fs.writeFileSync(cfgPath, '| language | 中文 |  # human-held, hands off\n');
    54	  const rulesPath = path.join(root, 'CLAUDE.md');
    55	  const rulesBefore = fs.readFileSync(rulesPath, 'utf8');
    56	  // one user-owned file in every protected surface the scenario names
    57	  const owned = {
    58	    'apriori/specs/kv.md': '#### Scenario: KV-01 x\n',
    59	    'apriori/changes/add-x/flow-state.md': '| step | STEP2 |\n',
    60	    'apriori/changes/add-x/review/issues.md': '| I1 | open |\n',
    61	    'apriori/truth/kv.md': 'KB fact\n',
    62	  };
    63	  for (const [rel, body] of Object.entries(owned)) {
    64	    const p = path.join(root, ...rel.split('/'));
    65	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
    66	    fs.writeFileSync(p, body);
    67	  }
    68	  const { actions } = update.run(root);
    69	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(cfgPath, 'utf8'), '| language | 中文 |  # human-held, hands off\n');
    70	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(rulesPath, 'utf8'), rulesBefore);
    71	  for (const [rel, body] of Object.entries(owned))
    72	    assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, ...rel.split('/')), 'utf8'), body);
    73	  // only the runbook copy + the one existing command file appear; no other tool's command was created
    74	  assert.deepStrictEqual(actions.map((a) => a.file).sort(), ['.claude/commands/apriori.md', 'apriori/runbook.md']);
    75	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(path.join(root, '.codex')));
    76	});
    77	
    78	test('UP-03 uninitialized project errors naming apriori init', () => {
    79	  const root = tmp();
    80	  assert.throws(() => update.run(root), /apriori init/);
    81	});
    82	
    83	test('UP-04 --dry-run previews without writing', () => {
    84	  const root = agedProject();
    85	  // through the CLI, as the scenario states: reports what would be refreshed, writes nothing
    86	  const { spawnSync } = require('node:child_process');
    87	  const r = spawnSync('node', [path.join(__dirname, '..', 'bin', 'apriori.js'), 'update', '--dry-run'],
    88	    { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8' });
    89	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0);
    90	  assert.match(r.stdout, /apriori\/runbook\.md\s+\(updated\)/);
    91	  assert.match(r.stdout, /would be refreshed/);
    92	  // both stale tool-owned files are untouched
    93	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md'), 'utf8'),
    94	    '# old runbook from a previous CLI version\n');
    95	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, '.claude', 'commands', 'apriori.md'), 'utf8'),
    96	    'old command body\n');
    97	});
    98	
    99	test('UP-05 protocol-required scaffolding is re-established', () => {
   100	  // a project initialized before the gitignored scratch dir existed
   101	  const root = agedProject();
   102	  fs.rmSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore'), { force: true });
   103	  fs.rmSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'tmp'), { recursive: true, force: true });
   104	  const { actions } = update.run(root);
   105	  assert.ok(actions.some((a) => a.file === 'apriori/.gitignore' && a.action === 'created'));
   106	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore'), 'utf8'), 'tmp/\n');
   107	  assert.ok(fs.statSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'tmp')).isDirectory());
   108	  // an existing (customized) .gitignore is never modified
   109	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore'), 'tmp/\ncustom/\n');
   110	  const again = update.run(root);
   111	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore'), 'utf8'), 'tmp/\ncustom/\n');
   112	  assert.ok(!again.actions.some((a) => a.file === 'apriori/.gitignore'));
   113	});

   115	// ---- update-manifest (UP-06..11): update refreshes only manifest-proven files ----

   117	test('UP-06 a foreign file at a tool path survives update', () => {
   118	  const root = agedProject();
   119	  const foreign = path.join(root, '.codex', 'prompts', 'apriori.md');
   120	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(foreign), { recursive: true });

MIGRATING.md:19:| unstamped MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED delta at the gate | passed silently | **C7 blocks by default** — run `apriori stamp <store-file>`, or waive visibly (`gate --no-cas`, or a `| cas | optional |` process-config row). verify/archive only warn this minor; stamps become mandatory in 4.0 |
RUNBOOK.md:187:- **CAS base stamps (serialize-rule tooling):** when authoring a delta, run `apriori stamp apriori/specs/<module>/spec.md` and paste the printed `<!-- apriori-base: … -->` line at the top of the delta file (before the first `## … Requirements` section; `new` for a not-yet-existing store). Both `verify --change` and `archive` then refuse if the store has diverged since the delta was authored — §4.11's serialize rule made mechanical. Enforcement is graded: unstamped MUTATION deltas (MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED) still merge but WARN on every surface and gate C7 denies them by default (`--no-cas` or a `| cas | optional |` config row waives it, visibly) — stamps become mandatory in 4.0; a stamped delta already fully applied re-runs cleanly (the mismatch downgrades to a rerun-accepted note).

审查结论：有 2 个需求缺陷，均是覆盖/验收边界问题；其余为可实现性或实现建议。

**Issues**

CM-1 — D8/update 旧布局检测漏掉顶层 `spike/`  
`req-v1.md:31` 只列了 `apriori/review/`、`apriori/design/`、`apriori/explore/`、顶层 `requirement/`。但 4.0 changelog 明确五个 scattered roots 为 `requirement/`、`spike/`、`apriori/review/`、`apriori/design/`、`apriori/explore/`（`CHANGELOG.md:7`），协议测试也按这五个根绑定（`test/protocol.test.js:466-473`）。  
风险：从 3.x explore/prototype 布局升级时，顶层 `spike/` 会被 doctor/update 静默放过，正好违背“对 3.x 旧布局不再沉默”的目标。  
Fix：Section 3 检测集改为五项：`requirement/`、`spike/`、`apriori/review/`、`apriori/design/`、`apriori/explore/`；MIGRATING 4.0 小节和 doctor/update 测试都覆盖五项，含 symlink root 按存在处理。

CM-2 — `status --change` 的路径保护只指定 change-dir resolver，不足以覆盖实际读文件面  
`req-v1.md:24-27` 要修复 symlink 逃逸读取，但 `gate.resolveChange()` 当前只验证变更目录或归档目录 containment（`lib/gate.js:23-45`）。`status` 实际还会读 `flow-state.md` 和 `review/issues.md`（现状在 `lib/status.js:43-47`），若 bundle 目录本身合法，但这两个文件是指向工作区外的 symlink，单靠 resolver 仍不能阻止外部文件内容被读取。doctor 对同类问题已有逐文件 containment 模式（`lib/doctor.js:143-147`, `163-167`）。  
风险：需求声称修复“symlink 可读工作区外文件”，但实现者按文面复用 resolver 后仍可能留下文件级逃逸。  
Fix：Section 2 明确：`status --change` 解析到 bundle 后，读取 `flow-state.md` 前必须验证该文件存在、为普通文件/或至少 realpath contained；`review/issues.md` 若存在也必须 contained 且非逃逸 symlink，否则 exit 2 具名错误。缺失 `flow-state.md` 建议也定为 exit 2；缺失 ledger 可继续按 0 open 处理，除非另行收紧。

**Advisories**

Section 1 可实现：两种 archive form 都能在 parse/merge 后、任何 temp/stage/write 前拒绝 unstamped mutation；`--no-cas` + `| cas | optional |` 与 C7 词汇一致，`verify --change` 保持 warn 是正确分层。

实现上不要让 `status` 直接 `require('./gate')` 取 `resolveChange()`，因为 `gate` 已经 `require('./status')`（`lib/gate.js:10`）。把 resolver 拆到共享模块，或调整依赖方向。`configCas` 同理，若 archive 也复用它，最好共享而不是制造环依赖。

`status` 无参列表保持只列 active 是合理的； archived detail 已由 `--change` 覆盖，默认列表混入 archive 会降低可扫读性。

**LEDGER DELTA**

```md
| CM-1 | D8/update legacy detection omits top-level `spike/`, despite 4.0 defining five legacy roots. | medium | 1 | open |
| CM-2 | `status --change` path protection is underspecified for file-level `flow-state.md` / `review/issues.md` symlink escapes after resolver succeeds. | high | 1 | open |
```

VERDICT: 2 issues found
